It records a ruling `R-n`, writes `R/exploration/returns/plan.md` (the unresolved objections with class, the plan and estimate hashes), and opens cycle `c+1` of P2; the P2 exit names P3 or P4 as for a slice return (UC-02 step 2.1), and P4 of the new cycle starts its round count at one. Each gate brief of the new cycle lists the returned objections of its role, and that role's pass must dispose of each (§5.3).
   6.1 Bound: one plan return per run. When round 5 of P4 ends again without every counter-role passing, `run.mjs return plan` refuses and the run halts with STOP-7 for the owner.

#### Error cases

| Case | Behavior |
|---|---|
| An AC appears in no slice or in two | `plan-check.mjs` fails with the AC id |
| A dependency cycle between slices | `plan-check.mjs` fails with the cycle |
| A brief lacks the `input-class block` where the slice declares the trigger | `brief-lint.mjs` fails with slice and missing classes |
| Estimate judged "nicht tragbar" with an irreversible commitment | STOP-1, owner |
| A counter-role still blocks after round 5 (for example the estimate still "nicht tragbar" on a reversible plan) | plan return to P2 (step 6); after the one return used up: STOP-7 |
| A sixth round spawn of a P4 gate counter-role | denied (AC-04.9) |

#### Acceptance criteria

| # | Criterion | Test type | Test class | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| AC-04.1 | An AC missing from every slice fails `plan-check.mjs` with its id | Unit | `checks/plan-check.test.mjs` "fires: orphan AC" | red |
| AC-04.2 | A cycle S-1 → S-2 → S-1 fails `plan-check.mjs` | Unit | `checks/plan-check.test.mjs` "fires: dependency cycle" | red |
| AC-04.3 | A brief with the trigger and a cell left empty in the `input-class block` fails `brief-lint.mjs` | Unit | `checks/brief-lint.test.mjs` "fires: empty input-class cell" | red |
| AC-04.4 | A brief whose objective text hash differs from the run's assignment hash fails `brief-lint.mjs` | Unit | `checks/brief-lint.test.mjs` "fires: paraphrased objective" | red |
| AC-04.5 | At `A1` `run.mjs exit refinement` leaves the phase `gate-pending` until `run.mjs approve refinement` is confirmed | Integration | `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: human gate at A1" | red |
| AC-04.6 | Two slices without a dependency path whose `paths touched` overlap fail `plan-check.mjs` with both slice ids and the path | Unit | `checks/plan-check.test.mjs` "fires: parallel slices share a file" | red |
| AC-04.7 | A slice whose `paths touched` lie under a `B-n` of kind `path` fails `plan-check.mjs` with slice and ban ids | Unit | `checks/plan-check.test.mjs` "fires: banned path planned" | red |
| AC-04.8 | A brief whose scope block names a `B-n` scope term fails `brief-lint.mjs` with the ban id | Unit | `checks/brief-lint.test.mjs` "fires: banned scope in brief" | red |
| AC-04.9 | A sixth round spawn of a P4 gate counter-role in one cycle is denied; a fifth is allowed | Unit | `hooks/guard-spawn.test.mjs` "fires: sixth plan round" | red |
| AC-04.10 | `run.mjs return plan` after round 5 with an open `pessimistic-realist` block writes `returns/plan.md`, opens P2 cycle 2, and `run.mjs enter implementation` is denied until P4 of cycle 2 exits | Integration | `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: implementation before plan return cycle exits" | red |
| AC-04.11 | A second `run.mjs return plan` in one run is refused and the run status becomes `HALT-ROUNDS` with STOP-7 | Unit | `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: second plan return" | red |

### UC-05: Run the implementation phase

**Actor:** `operator`, `orchestrator` or `team lead` with `impl-uc`, `impl-debug`, `impl-refactor`, `review-verify`, `review-code`.
**Route:** skill `cdp6-core:implement`; `run.mjs enter implementation`; per slice `worktree-setup.mjs`, `run.mjs worktree`, spawn, `integrate.mjs`; `run.mjs exit implementation`

#### Phase card

| Item | Content |
|---|---|
| Entry | P4 exited or imported, or P3 and P4 `skipped` by the bug-report route with P1 and P2 exited (UC-02 step 5.1); the sandbox check of §5.6 passes (settings, A-12 record for the current permission mode, and a live refused connection no older than ten minutes); on native Windows, which runs commands unsandboxed (sandboxing.md:13), `run.mjs enter implementation` refuses and names WSL2; per slice: every dependency slice `integrated` |
| Exit | every slice `integrated`, or `parked` by an owner ruling; no slice `returned` with its return cycle open; `plan-check.mjs --completion` exit 0 (every path-named plan item exists, GS plan-completion honesty) |
| Artefacts | worktree `.worktrees/<run-id>-<S-n>` on the base commit with green baseline; `R/reports/S-n.md`; `R/diffs/S-n.patch`; `R/tool-log.jsonl` (written by `trajectory.mjs`); integration commit on the run branch (at `A3`) or staged diff for the owner |
| Single | operator implements each slice test-first in its own worktree with the implement skill; in an active run in phase P5 with mode `single`, `guard-files.mjs` limits the main session's writes to the slice's worktree and `R/` (state files excluded), and `trajectory.mjs` logs the main session's tool calls (KR-8) |
| Multi | one fresh `impl-uc` (or `impl-debug`, `impl-refactor`) per slice; workers spawn no subagents; review in two stages: `review-verify` with the brief as source (the review brief reuses the slice's `input-class block`), then `review-code` when the diff adds a file (a new class or seam) or adds more than 150 lines (orchestrate SKILL.md:76; `git diff --numstat`, `--diff-filter=A`) |
| Team | one `impl-uc` teammate per slice in its own worktree (`writes:` that worktree and its report); teammates of parallel slices message `question` and `answer` about shared interfaces; reviews run as spoke subagents (C9t); re-briefs by `rebrief` message are counted like spawns |
| Gate per slice | deterministic: `decision-check.mjs` on every `D-n` the slice's brief, report or team log cites (a choice arising in P5 also needs its devils-advocate capture before `decided`, O3; r3-KR N11), `ban-check.mjs` on the slice diff (no added dependency or technology the owner banned, no path under a banned path, §6.5), `integrate.mjs` (refuses a vetoed slice; applies the diff to a tree holding nothing else, re-runs the brief's gate commands on the integrated tree, rolls back with `git apply -R --index` on red; O8), `tdd-order.mjs` (a failing test run precedes the first production write per AC; O9), `verdict-check.mjs` on the captured review verdicts; judgment: `review-verify`, `review-code` |
| Gate of the phase | deterministic: `plan-check.mjs --completion`, `trace-check.mjs`; judgment: none beyond the per-slice reviews |
| Anti-drift | at spawn `guard-spawn.mjs` diffs the cited ADR/spec paths between the worktree base and the orchestrator's HEAD (O7); a non-empty diff denies the spawn |
| Standalone | `run.mjs init --start implementation --import plan.md --import briefs/` |
| Human gate | every integration at `A0` and `A1` (`run.mjs approve S-n`); at `A2` only the final integration (§6.1); commit at `A3` only on the run branch; push never; an owner veto on `S-n` at every level |

#### Behavior

1. `run.mjs enter implementation` checks P4, the plan approval and the sandbox check of §5.6.
2. Per slice in dependency order: `worktree-setup.mjs` creates the worktree and runs setup and baseline inside the sandbox; `run.mjs worktree S-n <path>` then registers the worktree in `run.json` (the registry `guard-spawn.mjs` reads, AC-12.24) only when the path's real path lies under `<project root>/.worktrees/` (r5-TM T5-5) and `tool-log.jsonl` holds the `worktree-setup.mjs` line for that path with exit 0; a red baseline registers nothing and stops the slice (CH-48).
3. The orchestrator spawns the worker with the brief header; `guard-spawn.mjs` runs `brief-lint.mjs`, the base diff (O7) and the round counter.
4. The worker delivers diff and report through `SubagentHandback`; `check-handback.mjs` captures the report, checks acknowledgement, hedges, written-file claims (O1) and PII.
5. A harness notice that the worker still has background work is handled by the O2 rule (§5.5).
6. `review-verify` and, by size, `review-code` run; their verdicts are captured and validated by `verdict-check.mjs`.
   6.1 A refuted result gets a re-brief that cites the failed `F-P5-n` row; at most two re-briefs, both by fresh spawn; a third worker spawn for the slice is denied.
   6.2 Slice return (owner decision §0 item 13): when both re-briefs are used up and the result is still refuted, the slice does not wait for the owner. The orchestrator triages each captured `F-P5-n` of the slice as `intent` (the intent was misunderstood), `plan` (the plan was wrong) or `assumption` (an assumption about the code or the host was wrong) and runs `run.mjs return S-n --class <class per finding>`, which refuses while a P5 spawn of the run has no capture (r3-DA O-12) and otherwise records a ruling `R-n`, writes `R/exploration/returns/S-n.md`, sets the slice `returned`, sets every slice that depends on it `paused`, sets P5 `returned` and opens cycle `c+1` of P2 (§5.1); the phase-order guard then requires P2, then P3 or P4 as the P2 exit names, then P4, before P5 is entered again in cycle `c+1`. The other open slices keep their state and continue when P5 is re-entered; integrated slices stay integrated; the returned slice and the paused slices become `briefed` when P4 of cycle `c+1` exits with a brief for each written in cycle `c+1`, and `brief-lint.mjs` refuses a brief that cites a superseded `S-n` or a plan file of an earlier cycle, so a stale brief is never spawned (r5-KR G-J, r5-DA O5-4). `review-verify` checks the triage at the P2 gate.
   6.3 Bound: one return per slice. After the return the slice gets two re-briefs again; when they are used up a second time, `run.mjs return` refuses and the run halts with STOP-7 for the owner.
7. `integrate.mjs` runs `ban-check.mjs`, refuses a vetoed slice, applies and re-runs the gates; red rolls back.
   7.1 At `A0` and `A1` the integration waits for `run.mjs approve S-n` (owner command); at `A2` only the final integration waits.
8. `run.mjs exit implementation` checks every slice and runs `plan-check.mjs --completion`.

#### Error cases

| Case | Behavior |
|---|---|
| Cited ADR changed on the orchestrator's HEAD after the worktree base | spawn denied with the paths; rebase the worktree or inline the text (O7) |
| Worker wrote production code before a failing test | `tdd-order.mjs` fails with the AC; a stub swap afterwards is "mutation evidence, not the test-first red" (O9) |
| Gate red on the integrated tree | `integrate.mjs` rolls back with `git apply -R --index`, exit 1, slice back to `review` |
| Main tree holds other uncommitted changes | `integrate.mjs` refuses, exit 2 |
| Worker writes outside its worktree | `guard-files.mjs` denies (O13) |
| Three identical failing commands by one worker | `guard-shell.mjs` denies the fourth with contract rule 6 (O17) |
| Two re-briefs used up, result still refuted | slice return to P2 (step 6.2); after the one return used up again: STOP-7 |
| A finding without a triage class at `run.mjs return` | refused with the finding id |
| Slice diff adds a banned dependency or touches a banned path | `ban-check.mjs` fails with the `B-n`; slice back to `review` |
| Owner vetoed the slice | `integrate.mjs` refuses with the `V-n`; the run halts (STOP-10) |
| Sandbox settings incomplete, A-12 record missing for the mode, or no fresh refused connection | `run.mjs enter implementation` refuses and names the missing item |

#### Acceptance criteria

| # | Criterion | Test type | Test class | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| AC-05.1 | `integrate.mjs` on a red gate leaves `git status --porcelain` equal to the state before the apply | Integration | `scripts/integrate.test.mjs` "fires: rollback on red gate" | red |
| AC-05.2 | `integrate.mjs` refuses a main tree with an unrelated modified file | Integration | `scripts/integrate.test.mjs` "fires: dirty tree" | red |
| AC-05.3 | A spawn whose cited spec path differs between base and HEAD is denied with the path | Integration | `hooks/guard-spawn.test.mjs` "fires: base drift" | red |
| AC-05.4 | A tool log with a production write before the first failing test run of AC-x fails `tdd-order.mjs` | Unit | `checks/tdd-order.test.mjs` "fires: code before red" | red |
| AC-05.5 | A third worker spawn for the same slice in one cycle is denied unless an owner ruling (`run.mjs rule --human --allow-round S-n`) allows it | Unit | `hooks/guard-spawn.test.mjs` "fires: third re-brief" | red |
| AC-05.6 | An `impl-uc` Write to a path outside its registered worktree is denied | Unit | `hooks/guard-files.test.mjs` "fires: write outside env" | red |
| AC-05.7 | A plan item naming a path that does not exist fails `plan-check.mjs --completion` | Unit | `checks/plan-check.test.mjs` "fires: path item not done" | red |
| AC-05.8 | `activity-check.mjs` reports `interim` while a process with its working directory in the worktree runs, and `final` after it ends with no file changed since the handback time | Integration | `scripts/activity-check.test.mjs` "fires: process in worktree" | red |
| AC-05.9 | `run.mjs grant` on a worktree with `git status --porcelain` output exits 2 | Integration | `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: grant on dirty root" | red |

| AC-05.12 | `run.mjs enter implementation` refuses, naming the key, for each settings fixture that misses one requirement of §5.6 (sandbox off, `failIfUnavailable` not true, `allowUnsandboxedCommands` not false, `strictAllowlist` not true, an `excludedCommands` entry other than the exact `run.mjs` pattern, a bare `*` in `allowedDomains` or in a `WebFetch(domain:…)` allow rule, no `denyWrite` on a run file or a plugin root, no credentials deny on `~/.ssh`, `~/.aws` or `~/.config/gh`), for the same keys set only in project settings, and on `process.platform` `win32` | Unit | `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: sandbox without strict allowlist", "fires: sandbox keys from project settings", "fires: webfetch wildcard domain" | red |

| AC-05.21 | `run.mjs init`, `run.mjs enter`, `run.mjs grant` and `cdp6-doctor` refuse when `allowUnsandboxedCommands` false comes only from user settings and project or local settings hold a key of sandboxing.md:761-766 (`excludedCommands`, `filesystem.allowWrite`) | Unit | `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: user-only strict mode with repo excludedCommands", "fires: repo allowWrite under user-only strict" | red |
| AC-05.22 | A slice citing `D-4` that is `decided` before its devils-advocate capture fails the per-slice gate with `decision-check.mjs` | Unit | `checks/decision-check.test.mjs` "fires: P5 choice before counter-role" | red |
| AC-05.23 | `run.mjs return S-1` while a P5 spawn of the run has no capture is refused with the agent id | Unit | `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: return with uncaptured spawn" | red |

| AC-05.17 | `run.mjs return S-1` after two refuted re-briefs writes `returns/S-1.md`, sets S-1 `returned`, opens P2 cycle 2, and `run.mjs enter implementation` is then denied until P4 of cycle 2 exits | Integration | `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: implementation before return cycle exits" | red |
| AC-05.18 | A second `run.mjs return S-1` is refused and the run status becomes `HALT-ROUNDS` with STOP-7 | Unit | `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: second return" | red |

| AC-05.20 | Live: an `impl-uc` Bash write to `R/verdicts/x.md` fails with a permission error from the sandbox | Live | `docs/cdp6/live-checks/<version>.md` row "run files denyWrite" | red |

### UC-06: Run the tests and verification phase

**Actor:** `operator`, `orchestrator` or `team lead` with `adversarial-auditor`, `review-verify`, `threat-modeler`; `owner` for relaxed tests.
**Route:** skill `cdp6-core:verify`; `run.mjs enter verification` … `run.mjs exit verification`

#### Phase card

| Item | Content |
|---|---|
| Entry | P5 exited or imported; for a granted auditor run the sandbox check of §5.6 |
| Exit | `trace-check.mjs --coverage` exit 0; `relax-check.mjs` exit 0 or every listed relaxation decided by the owner with `run.mjs relax <path>:<line> accept|reject --why "<why>"` (counted only from `run.json`); drift checks exit 0; `adversarial-auditor` verdict pass or pass-with-conditions on the integrated tree |
| Artefacts | `R/verification/trace-map.md` (`AC-nn.m` → test names → result, with the sha256 of the test log it was read from; ST trace map); `R/verification/test-log.txt` (full output of the gate commands on the integrated tree, hashed); `R/verification/relaxations.md` (every skipped, focused, expected-to-fail, snapshot-updated or threshold-lowered test in the run's diff, with the owner's decision; gstack gate integrity); `R/verification/findings.md` (`F-P6-n`) |
| Single | operator runs the gates on the integrated tree and writes the `trace map`; the auditor still runs as a subagent |
| Multi | `adversarial-auditor` with a run grant in a worktree on the integrated commit (counter-tests); `review-verify` on claims; `threat-modeler` with security share |
| Team | not allowed: every role here judges the team's own work (C9t) |
| Gate | deterministic: `trace-check.mjs --coverage` (every AC has at least one test that passed in the hashed test log), `relax-check.mjs`, `konzept-mapper` and `roadmap-drift` (cdp5-gates), `secrets-scan` and `pii-scan` (cdp5-gates), and the project's own SAST and dependency-scan commands from the plan's gate commands when the concept has a security share (doctrine §33.5); judgment: `adversarial-auditor`, whose brief also asks for injected instructions in the diff (TM residual) |
| Anti-drift | the acceptance verdict is forced to `rejected` while any slice is not `integrated` (BMAD); the `trace map` reads test names from the log, not from the reports |
| Standalone | `run.mjs init --start verification --import konzept.md --import <commit>` |
| Human gate | at `A0`; every relaxation at every level |

#### Behavior

1. `run.mjs enter verification` checks P5.
2. The executor runs the gate commands of all slices once on the integrated tree and stores the log with its hash.
3. `trace-check.mjs --coverage` builds the `trace map` from the log and the AC ids in test names.
4. `relax-check.mjs` lists relaxations in the run's diff; each goes to the owner.
5. Drift and scan checkers run; `adversarial-auditor` runs counter-tests in its granted worktree.
6. `run.mjs exit verification` records the exit.

#### Error cases

| Case | Behavior |
|---|---|
| An AC has no test name carrying its id | `trace-check.mjs --coverage` fails with the AC |
| A test was marked skip in the run's diff | `relax-check.mjs` lists it; exit only after the owner's decision |
| The test log was edited after its hash was taken | hash mismatch, gate fails |
| Auditor needs a run and no grant was issued | auditor reports "counter-test not executable"; the orchestrator asks the owner for the grant |
| Secret found by `secrets-scan` | STOP-2 |

#### Acceptance criteria

| # | Criterion | Test type | Test class | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| AC-06.1 | An AC whose only test failed in the log fails `trace-check.mjs --coverage` | Unit | `checks/trace-check.test.mjs` "fires: AC without passing test" | red |
| AC-06.2 | A diff adding `it.skip` is listed by `relax-check.mjs` with file and line | Unit | `checks/relax-check.test.mjs` "fires: added skip" | red |
| AC-06.3 | A lowered coverage threshold in a config file is listed by `relax-check.mjs` | Unit | `checks/relax-check.test.mjs` "fires: lowered threshold" | red |
| AC-06.4 | `run.mjs exit verification` with a slice in state `parked` records acceptance `rejected` unless the owner overrides with `run.mjs accept-rejected --why "<why>"` | Integration | `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: forced rejected" | red |
| AC-06.5 | A relaxation decision written into `relaxations.md` by Edit, without a `run.mjs relax` record in `run.json`, leaves `relax-check.mjs` failing; `run.mjs relax /etc/hosts:1 accept --why x` exits 2 naming the path rule of UC-09 step 1.4 | Unit | `checks/relax-check.test.mjs` "fires: relaxation decided outside run.json"; `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: relax path outside project" | red |

### UC-07: Run the retro phase

**Actor:** `operator`, `orchestrator` or `team lead` with `pm-retro` and `review-verify`.
**Route:** skill `cdp6-core:retro`; `run.mjs enter retro` … `run.mjs exit retro`

#### Phase card

| Item | Content |
|---|---|
| Entry | P6 exited, imported or `skipped` by the not-reproducible route, or the run is `ABANDONED` (an abandoned run also gets a retro, without `resume`); a run in `HALT-*` or `PARKED` is first resumed (`run.mjs resume`) or abandoned (`run.mjs abandon`), and its halts `H-n` appear in the retro either way (`loop-check.mjs --retro`) |
| Exit | `R/retro/retro.md` filed; then `retro-memory-loop-check` (cdp5-gates) exit 0 (O11); `loop-check.mjs --retro` exit 0 (it also requires every slice return, ban and veto of the run to appear); `review-verify` pass on carry-over statuses and recurrence counts |
| Artefacts | `R/retro/retro-brief.md` (git history as pasted commands with exit codes, O10); `R/retro/retro.md` (doctrine §32.6 layout: assignment verbatim, carry-over, decision chronicle, root causes with Five Whys and recurrence count, Kaizen table `K-n` with owner, measurable criterion and place, lessons `L-n`, fork balance §32.6.1); memory files per doctrine §32.6 |
| Single | operator writes the retro with the retro skill |
| Multi | `pm-retro` (read-only tools; the git history comes pasted in the brief) |
| Team | not allowed: one author; the team log `R/team-log.jsonl` is an input of the retro |
| Gate | deterministic: `retro-memory-loop-check` after the retro is filed, `loop-check.mjs --retro` (every `K-n` has owner, criterion and place; fork balance section present with one state per opened path; every halt `H-n` and every `R-n` of class `taste` appears); judgment: `review-verify` on the factual claims (carry-over statuses, recurrence counts, the closed/abandoned/open state of every opened path with its evidence); the fork-balance judgment itself (which fork should not exist) stays with `pm-retro` and, at `A0`, the owner (devils-advocate does not run in retros, doctrine §32.25 exclusions) |
| Anti-drift | the retro cites `F-`, `R-`, `H-` and `D-` ids from the run files, read from disk |
| Standalone | `run.mjs init --start retro --import ledger.md` |
| Human gate | at `A0` (§6.1: from `A1` the retro runs unattended) |

#### Behavior

1. `run.mjs enter retro` checks P6 exit (or its `imported` or `skipped` state) or the run status `ABANDONED`; in `HALT-*` or `PARKED` it refuses and names `resume` and `abandon`. In an `ABANDONED` run P7 and P8 run as usual and P8's exit keeps the status `ABANDONED` (§5.1).
2. The orchestrator writes `retro-brief.md` with `git log`, `git show --stat` and `git diff --stat` output pasted with their exit codes.
3. `pm-retro` (or the operator) writes the retro, opening with the previous retro's carry-over.
4. After filing, `retro-memory-loop-check` runs; a broken slug is fixed by creating it or by a known-issue line, never by deleting the citation (O11).
5. `review-verify` checks the carry-over and recurrence claims.
6. `run.mjs exit retro` records the exit.

#### Error cases

| Case | Behavior |
|---|---|
| Retro cites a memory slug that does not exist | `retro-memory-loop-check` exit 1; fix by creating the slug or a known-issue line |
| A citation was deleted to make the check pass | `loop-check.mjs --retro` compares the cited slug set before and after the fix and fails on a removed slug (O11) |
| `K-n` without owner or criterion | `loop-check.mjs --retro` fails with the id |
| First retro of a repository (nothing to carry over) | carry-over line `none: first retro`; `retro-memory-loop-check` has nothing to check and the line says so (field report item 11) |
| Retro brief without exit codes on the git commands | `brief-lint.mjs` fails (O10) |

#### Acceptance criteria

| # | Criterion | Test type | Test class | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| AC-07.1 | A retro brief with a `git log` block lacking an `exit:` line fails `brief-lint.mjs` | Unit | `checks/brief-lint.test.mjs` "fires: git history without exit" | red |
| AC-07.2 | Removing a cited slug between two runs of `loop-check.mjs --retro` fails with the slug | Unit | `checks/loop-check.test.mjs` "fires: deleted citation" | red |
| AC-07.3 | A `K-n` without measurable criterion fails `loop-check.mjs --retro` | Unit | `checks/loop-check.test.mjs` "fires: Kaizen without criterion" | red |
| AC-07.4 | `run.mjs exit retro` before `retro-memory-loop-check` ran on the filed retro is refused | Integration | `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: memory loop not run" | red |
| AC-07.5 | On a `run.json` fixture with status `HALT-STOP`, `run.mjs enter retro` is refused naming `resume` and `abandon`; with status `ABANDONED` it is allowed, and `run.mjs exit loop-back` leaves the status `ABANDONED` | Unit | `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: retro of a halted run without resume or abandon" | red |

### UC-08: Loop back to exploration

**Actor:** `operator` or `orchestrator`; `devils-advocate` on routing choices; `owner` starts the next run.
**Route:** skill `cdp6-core:loop-back`; `run.mjs enter loop-back` … `run.mjs exit loop-back`; `run.mjs init --from <previous run>`

#### Phase card

| Item | Content |
|---|---|
| Entry | P7 exited or imported |
| Exit | `loop-check.mjs --forward` and `decision-check.mjs` exit 0; `devils-advocate` verdict on the routing when a route other than `defer` is chosen, captured before the routing is stamped `decided`; the ledger's `choices:` line present, and when it is not `none` a devils-advocate capture on it, when it is `none` a `review-verify` capture whose brief carries the undeclared-choice duty (OR-29) |
| Artefacts | `R/loop-back/routes.md` (every open `K-n` and `L-n` with a route that uses the triage classes of the in-run slice return (UC-05 step 6.2): `intent` → P1, `plan` → P4, `assumption` → P2, plus `patch` → P5 of a follow-up run, `explore` → P2, `defer` with owner and date; BMAD triage; every slice return of the finished run is listed with its class and outcome); `R/loop-back/next-run.md` (draft assignment and the `Q-n` candidates, each citing its `K-n`/`L-n`); usage-log triggers (third run of a manual activity) |
| Single and multi | the operator or orchestrator routes; routing is a choice and goes to `devils-advocate` |
| Team | not allowed: one author |
| Gate | deterministic: `loop-check.mjs --forward` (every open `K-n`/`L-n` routed; `defer` has owner and date; every `explore` route yields a `Q-n` candidate citing it; usage-log activities at their third run are listed); judgment: `devils-advocate` |
| Anti-drift | the next run's `exploration/questions.md` imports the candidates by id; `sources-check.mjs` fails a next run whose questions drop a routed candidate without a `defer` record |
| Standalone | `run.mjs init --start loop-back --import retro.md --import usage-log.jsonl` |
| Human gate | the next run starts on the owner's go at every level, except `A3` with a loop budget greater than zero, no web `SRC-n` in the finished run and an intake origin `conversation` (a run that read web content or a tracker or URL intake does not seed the next run unattended: cross-run poisoning, TM2-7) (CH-16); open bans carry into the next run |

#### Behavior

1. `run.mjs enter loop-back` checks P7.
2. Each open `K-n` and `L-n` gets a route; a recurring root cause at its third recurrence (a 🔴 cause at its first) becomes a decision proposal for the owner.
3. `loop-check.mjs --forward` reads the usage log and lists manual activities at their third run (doctrine §32.7 trigger). A manual activity enters the log only through `run.mjs usage <activity>` (a routine subcommand, UC-11 step 1.2), because `<data>/` is write-denied for every caller (r5-DA O5-3).
4. `next-run.md` is written; the run ends `DONE` (an `ABANDONED` run keeps its status, UC-07 step 1).
5. `run.mjs init --from <run>` of the next run imports the candidates; at `A3` with loop budget the init runs unattended and the budget decreases by one.

#### Error cases

| Case | Behavior |
|---|---|
| An open `K-n` without route | `loop-check.mjs --forward` fails with the id |
| Next run's questions omit a routed candidate | the next run's `sources-check.mjs` fails with the candidate id |
| Loop budget zero at `A3` | run ends `DONE`; the next run waits for the owner |
| A `K-n` route outside the enum | `loop-check.mjs --forward` fails with the id and the allowed routes |
| Usage log unreadable | `loop-check.mjs` exit 2, `fail closed` |

#### Acceptance criteria

| # | Criterion | Test type | Test class | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| AC-08.1 | An open `K-n` missing from `routes.md` fails `loop-check.mjs --forward` | Unit | `checks/loop-check.test.mjs` "fires: unrouted Kaizen" | red |
| AC-08.2 | Three main-session calls `run.mjs usage test-perf` write three `manual:test-perf` lines into `<data>/usage-log.jsonl`, and `loop-check.mjs --forward` on that log yields a trigger line for `test-perf` | Unit | `scripts/run.test.mjs` "records manual usage line"; `checks/loop-check.test.mjs` "fires: third manual run" | red |
| AC-08.3 | A next run whose questions drop a routed candidate fails `sources-check.mjs` | Integration | `checks/sources-check.test.mjs` "fires: dropped loop-back candidate" | red |
| AC-08.4 | `run.mjs init --from` at `A3` with loop budget 0 refuses to start without the owner | Integration | `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: loop budget exhausted" | red |
| AC-08.5 | `run.mjs exit loop-back` refuses a ledger without a `choices:` line, a non-empty `choices:` line without a devils-advocate capture, and `choices: none` without a captured `review-verify` verdict whose brief carries the undeclared-choice duty | Integration | `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: choices line unchecked", "fires: choices none unchecked" | red |
| AC-08.6 | `run.mjs init --from` at `A3` refuses unattended start when the previous run holds a web `SRC-n` | Integration | `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: web source seeds next run" | red |
| AC-08.7 | `run.mjs init --from` at `A3` refuses unattended start when the previous run's intake origin is not `conversation` | Integration | `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: tracker intake seeds next run" | red |
| AC-08.8 | `run.mjs init --from` copies every open `B-n` of the previous run into the new `run.json` | Integration | `scripts/run.test.mjs` "carries open bans" | red |

### UC-09: Initialise, resume and inspect a run

**Actor:** `orchestrator` or `operator`; `cdp6-hooks` (bootstrap).
**Route:** `node <P>/scripts/run.mjs init|status|resume`; SessionStart hook `bootstrap.mjs`

#### Behavior

1. `run.mjs init --intake <file> --kind idea|feature-request|issue|bug-report --origin <tracker id, URL or conversation> --class own|company [--root <path>] --mode single|multi|team [--assignment <file>] [--start <phase> --import <file>…]` creates the `run directory`, `run.json`, `ledger.md`, `counters.json` and the pointer in `<data>/active-runs.json`, which binds the hook input's `session_id` to the run (§1.4 item 2); `run.mjs resume` re-binds the resuming session. Transport of the session id (r4-KR G-1, r4-TM T4-2): for every `run.mjs` call `guard-shell.mjs` returns `updatedInput` with `--session-id <session_id of the hook input>` and `--data-dir <CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA of the hook process>` appended (hooks.md:1962), with the keyed `--hook-token` (UC-11 step 1.3), together with its `ask` or `allow` (UC-11 steps 1, 1.2), and denies a call that already carries any token starting with `--session-id`, `--data-dir`, `--hook-token` or the v0.4.5 `--argv-sha256` (r5-TM T5-4, r5-DA O5-2, cc-TM F6-1, cc2-TM F7-1); `run.mjs` exits 2 unless each of the three occurs exactly once, `init` and `resume` bind only that session value, and `run.mjs` reads no session-pointer file and no data-dir pointer file (CDP5's shell scripts resolve the directory through a host-wide pointer, `cdp5-agents/lib/data-dir.mjs:1-6`, `:37-40`).
   1.1 `--start` other than `idea` records the imported files and their hashes as the predecessor exit, behind a permission `ask` that lists each file with its sha256 (UC-11 step 1).
   1.2 `--mode team` (an owner command) requires the probe record `<data>/probes/<claude-version>.json` with A-11 and A-16 passed and A-14 or A-17 passed; otherwise `init` refuses, names the failed probe and points to `--mode multi`, as after a `team fault` (§3.4 item 12).
   1.3 `init` refuses below Claude Code 2.1.295 (§1.4 item 9) and without the sandbox requirements of §5.6, including its scope rule (admin-required sandbox, or no loosening key in project and local settings) and the `--settings` rules (location and recorded sha256): the sandbox bounds the main session's Bash for the whole run, not only P5 (TM2-3, r3-TM TM3-1, r4-TM T4-1). From SL-1 it also refuses when `~/.claude/settings.json` lacks the deny rules on the host key (§5.6 item 9, AC-11.17) or on the transcript directory `~/.claude/projects/` (§5.6 item 9, AC-11.18), or the `Edit` and `Write` deny rules on the run files and the journal of the run root it creates, on `<data>/` and on the installed plugin root (§5.6 item 10, AC-09.15); this narrow read of user settings comes before the full §5.6 check of SL-4.
   1.4 Path arguments (r4-TM T4-3, r5-KR G-G, r5-TM T5-5): `run.mjs` runs unsandboxed (§5.6 item 6), so every subcommand resolves each path argument (`--intake`, `--assignment`, `--import`, `--root`, `--file`, `--from`, `--objections`, the file of `probe --accept`, and the positional paths of `worktree S-n <path>` and `relax <path>:<line>`) to its real path and refuses one outside the project root, the run's `R` and the run roots of the project's earlier runs, one matching the secret patterns of UC-13 step 1.1, one under the host-key directory `~/.config/cdp6-core/`, refused by name whatever the project root (cc3-TM D-5), and one under a credentials deny entry of the counted settings (§5.6 item 5). Exceptions: `<data>/usage-log.jsonl` is accepted as a read-only `--import` (UC-08 Standalone); an `--import` that is no path is resolved as a commit with `git rev-parse --verify <value>^{commit}` in the project (UC-06 Standalone); the `worktree` path must lie under `<project root>/.worktrees/` (UC-05 step 2).
2. Every write to `run.json` goes through `lib/run-store.mjs`: lock file, journal line, atomic rename (`single writer`).
3. At SessionStart with source `startup`, `resume`, `clear` or `compact` (`cdp5-agents/hooks/hooks.json:5`, `bootstrap.mjs:32-38` read `source` and `session_id`), `bootstrap.mjs` prints the active run: id, phase, phase state, next allowed action, path of the ledger.
4. `run.mjs status` prints the same from disk; after a compaction the session resumes from it, not from memory.
5. `run.mjs id <kind>` allocates the next id under the run lock (§3.4); `run.mjs id --map <agent-id>:<kind>-n` maps a provisional id.
6. `run.mjs resume` (behind an `ask`) returns a halted run to `active`; in team mode it marks the roster `lost` (§3.4 item 10). `run.mjs repair` (behind an `ask`) replays the journal into `run.json`.

#### Error cases

| Case | Behavior |
|---|---|
| `run.json` unreadable or journal and file disagree | `run.mjs` exit 2; hooks deny every CDP6 action for that project with the reason (`fail closed`); `run.mjs repair` replays the journal behind a permission `ask` |
| Lock held by a crashed writer older than the lock timeout | lock broken with a journal line; younger lock: exit 2 |
| Second active run for the same project root | `init` refuses; one active run per project root |
| Company repository and `R` under the repository | `init` refuses (no AI files in company repositories) |

#### Acceptance criteria

| # | Criterion | Test type | Test class | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| AC-09.1 | Two concurrent `run.mjs` writers leave `run.json` valid and the journal with both lines | Integration | `lib/run-store.test.mjs` "fires: concurrent writers" | red |
| AC-09.2 | A truncated `run.json` makes `guard-phase.mjs` deny with exit-2 semantics | Unit | `hooks/guard-phase.test.mjs` "fires: unreadable run record" | red |
| AC-09.3 | `bootstrap.mjs` with source `compact` prints run id, phase and next action | Unit | `hooks/bootstrap.test.mjs` "prints run status after compact" | red |
| AC-09.4 | `init --class company` with `R` inside the repository exits 2 | Unit | `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: company repo path" | red |
| AC-09.5 | Two concurrent `run.mjs id D` calls return two different ids | Integration | `lib/run-store.test.mjs` "fires: duplicate id under concurrency" | red |
| AC-09.6 | `run.mjs repair` on a `run.json` truncated after the last journal line restores the journal state | Integration | `lib/run-store.test.mjs` "repairs from journal" | red |
| AC-09.7 | `init --mode team` without a `<data>/probes/<claude-version>.json` record for the current Claude Code version that shows A-11, A-16 and A-14 or A-17 passed exits 2 | Unit | `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: team without probes" | red |
| AC-09.8 | `init --root docs/records` in an own repository whose CLAUDE.md and README do not name that path exits 2 | Unit | `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: unnamed record place" | red |
| AC-09.9 | `init` with a stubbed `claude --version` of 2.1.294 exits 2 naming the floor | Unit | `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: below version floor" | red |
| AC-09.10 | `init` with `sandbox.network.strictAllowlist` set only in `.claude/settings.json` of the project exits 2 | Unit | `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: run without user-level sandbox" | red |
| AC-09.11 | `run.mjs init` without `--session-id`, or with two, exits 2; a model-written `run.mjs init … --session-id x` and `run.mjs init … --session-id=x` are denied by `guard-shell.mjs`, and a call without it gets `updatedInput` with the hook input's `session_id` appended; with a CDP5-style session-pointer file naming another live session, `init` binds only the `--session-id` value | Unit | `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: init without session id", "fires: duplicate session id", "fires: init binds pointer session of another live session"; `hooks/guard-shell.test.mjs` "fires: forged session binding", "fires: --session-id=x form" | red |

| AC-09.14 | Every `run.mjs` call gets `updatedInput` with `--data-dir` set to the hook's `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA`; a call that carries its own `--data-dir` is denied by `guard-shell.mjs`; `run.mjs` without exactly one `--data-dir` exits 2, and with a pointer file `~/.cache/cdp6-core/data-dir` naming a forged directory it reads the probe record of the `--data-dir` directory | Unit | `hooks/guard-shell.test.mjs` "fires: forged data dir"; `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: data dir from pointer file" | red |
| AC-09.15 | `run.mjs init` with a user-settings fixture that lacks an `Edit` or a `Write` deny rule covering `run.json`, `run.journal.jsonl`, `counters.json` or `verdicts/` of the run root it would create, `<data>/**` or the installed plugin root `<P>/**` (cc3-TM D-2) exits 2 naming the missing rule; with all twelve rules it proceeds | Unit | `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: run files editable by Edit tool", "fires: journal writable by Write tool", "fires: nonce record writable by Write tool", "fires: plugin lib editable by Edit tool" | red |

### UC-10: Guard the phase order

**Actor:** `cdp6-hooks` (`guard-phase.mjs`).
**Route:** PreToolUse hook on `Agent|Task|Bash|PowerShell|Monitor|Edit|Write|NotebookEdit` for its decisions (Monitor runs shell commands, §1.4 item 10), and on PreToolUse, PermissionRequest and PermissionDenied with the catch-all matcher `"*"` (hooks.md:279) for the guard line and the permission-mode check, while a run is active

#### Behavior

1. The hook finds the active run by the lookup of §1.4 item 2 (`session_id` binding, then `agent_id` binding, then a run root or project root that is a prefix of `cwd` or of the target path; a linked worktree maps to its project through `git rev-parse --git-common-dir`); a missing pointer with an active `run.json` in the project's run roots denies; no active run → no decision.
2. A spawn of a `cdp6-core:` role: the brief header's `phase:` equals the run's entered phase and the role is allowed in it (role map in `lib/registry.mjs`); otherwise deny. The role map allows `konzept-reviewer` as outside reviewer in every phase with a `D-n` (the phases whose gate runs `decision-check.mjs`: P1, P3, P4, P5, P8; §10.1 row O3; C13).
3. A shell command calling `run.mjs enter <phase>`: the predecessor has an exit record (`exited`, `imported`, or `skipped` by the bug-report route); otherwise deny. `run.mjs` checks the same (defence in depth).
4. A file write into `R/<phase directory>/` of a phase that is not entered: deny.
5. Every guard entry in `hooks.json` carries `"onFailure": "block"`: a hook "with a wrong path or a crashing script then lets everything through. To block the action instead, set "onFailure": "block"" (hooks.md:904; values hooks.md:447, :499; Claude Code v2.1.295 or later, host 2.1.311 per r1-team-facts). This covers a missing script, a missing `node`, a crash at module load and a timeout (hooks.md:784, :879). The gstack shell form of v0.1 is dropped (CH-7).
6. Every firing appends a `hooks observed` line (hook name, plugin version, time, `tool_use_id`, `permission_mode`, decision) to `<data>/hooks-fired.jsonl`; the catch-all registration writes such a line for every tool call of a run, also for tools no named guard matches, for every `PermissionRequest` (tool name and input hash, no `tool_use_id`, hooks.md:2085) and for every `PermissionDenied` of auto mode (with `tool_use_id`, hooks.md:2406) (r3-DA O-10, O-7, r4-DA N6). The PermissionRequest and PermissionDenied registrations only observe: they return no decision, never `allow` (a PermissionRequest hook could allow for the owner, hooks.md:1210; AC-10.12).
7. Permission mode (owner decision §0 item 11): while a run is active, every CDP6 PreToolUse guard denies a tool call whose `permission_mode` is `bypassPermissions` (and plan mode with bypass available), for every caller; the catch-all registration makes this cover every tool. A missing `permission_mode` is denied the same way until probe A-13 shows the field.
8. A shell command calling `run.mjs enter <next phase>` or `run.mjs exit <phase>` is denied while a veto `V-n` on that exit is open (`run.mjs` checks the same).

#### Error cases

| Case | Behavior |
|---|---|
| Hook script deleted, `node` missing, a required lib missing, or the hook times out | `onFailure: "block"` blocks the action; no CDP6 action passes |
| `run.json` unreadable | deny with the reason |
| Brief header without `phase:` | deny, reason names the missing line |
| Main session writes `run.json` with Edit | denied by `guard-files.mjs` (`single writer`, UC-13 step 1.1, AC-13.3, built in SL-4); `guard-phase.mjs` denies it only as part of the missing-pointer deny of step 1 (AC-10.6) |

#### Acceptance criteria

| # | Criterion | Test type | Test class | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| AC-10.1 | A spawn of `cdp6-core:impl-uc` while the run is in `concept` is denied | Unit | `hooks/guard-phase.test.mjs` "fires: role outside phase" | red |
| AC-10.2 | Every guard entry of `hooks.json` has `"onFailure": "block"`; removing it from one entry fails the test | Unit | `hooks/hooks-json.test.mjs` "fires: guard without onFailure block" | red |
| AC-10.3 | Each firing appends one line to `hooks-fired.jsonl` | Unit | `hooks/guard-phase.test.mjs` "records hooks observed" | red |
| AC-10.4 | Without an active run the hook returns no decision for the main session | Unit | `hooks/guard-phase.test.mjs` "silent without run" | red |
| AC-10.5 | `run.mjs enter` refuses when `hooks-fired.jsonl` has no `guard-phase.mjs` line since the session start | Unit | `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: guard not observed" | red |
| AC-10.6 | With `active-runs.json` removed while a `run.json` says `active`, a main-session Edit of `run.json` is denied; the same from a `cwd` inside a linked worktree | Unit | `hooks/guard-phase.test.mjs` "fires: pointer missing with active run", "fires: worktree cwd" | red |
| AC-10.7 | Live (SL-0): with a lib the probe hook imports renamed and with `node` removed from PATH, under a catch-all registration with `onFailure: "block"` a Bash call, an Edit and a Read are blocked and a permission request is denied, also in a session without a run | Live | probe record row "onFailure block" | red |
| AC-10.8 | In an active run a Read with `permission_mode: "bypassPermissions"` is denied by `guard-files.mjs`, a Bash call by `guard-shell.mjs` and an Agent call by `guard-phase.mjs` | Unit | `hooks/guard-phase.test.mjs` "fires: bypass mode in run" and the same title in the other two test files | red |
| AC-10.9 | `run.mjs exit idea` with an open `V-1` on `exit:idea` is refused and the run status becomes `HALT-STOP` with STOP-10 | Unit | `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: vetoed exit" | red |
| AC-10.10 | With the run bound to the input's `session_id`: a main-session `run.mjs enter concept` out of order from a `cwd` inside a nested repository is denied by `guard-phase.mjs`; `run.mjs approve` from a foreign `cwd` returns `ask` | Unit | `hooks/guard-phase.test.mjs` "fires: nested repo cwd"; `hooks/guard-shell.test.mjs` "fires: approve from foreign cwd" | red |
| AC-10.11 | In an active run a tool call whose name no named guard matches gets a `hooks-fired.jsonl` line from the catch-all registration with its `tool_use_id`, and is denied under `bypassPermissions` | Unit | `hooks/guard-phase.test.mjs` "fires: unmatched tool unobserved" | red |
| AC-10.12 | Over every fixture of `hooks/guard-phase.test.mjs`, the output of the PermissionRequest and PermissionDenied registrations carries no `allow` decision | Unit | `hooks/guard-phase.test.mjs` "fires: permission observer returns allow" | red |
| AC-10.13 | In an active run in each of P1, P3, P4, P5 and P8, a spawn of `cdp6-core:konzept-reviewer` whose brief header names the entered phase passes the role-map check of `guard-phase.mjs` | Unit | `hooks/guard-phase.test.mjs` "allows outside reviewer in decision phases" | red |

### UC-11: Run unattended within an autonomy level

**Actor:** `orchestrator`; `owner` sets the level and answers stops.
**Route:** `run.mjs set-autonomy A0|A1|A2|A3 --tokens <n> --loops <n>`; `run.mjs rule`; `run.mjs halt`

#### Behavior

1. The owner sets the `autonomy level` (§6.1) and budgets; the permission `ask` on the command is the human confirmation. Owner commands: `approve <phase|S-n>`, `set-autonomy`, `resume`, `repair`, `rule --human`, `judge`, `init --start`, `init --mode team`, `team`, `allow-url`, `allow-search`, `ban`, `ban --lift`, `veto`, `veto --lift`, `probe --accept <file>`, `object <F-Pk-n> accept|reject --why "<why>"`, `relax <path>:<line> accept|reject --why "<why>"`, `accept-rejected --why "<why>"`, `abandon --why "<why>"`, `grant` (CH-48), and `decision D-n decided` with a decider other than the executor role (r3-KR N1, N5). Each writes a stamped owner record (decider `owner`, ISO time) into `run.json`; `decision-check.mjs`, `relax-check.mjs` and `run.mjs` count owner records only from `run.json`, never from a Markdown file (AC-03.10, AC-06.5). `guard-shell.mjs` returns `ask` for each, with the prompt reason built from the parsed arguments only (never from free text of the command); for a command that takes a file (`allow-url --file`, `ban --file`, `judge --objections`, `init --import`, `probe --accept`) the reason lists every file with its sha256 (for `allow-url --file` also every URL), and the hook returns `updatedInput` with one `--expect-sha256 <hash>` per file appended (hooks.md:1962: "Combine with … "ask" to show the modified input to the user"), so `run.mjs` refuses when a file changed after the approval (r3-TM TM3-4); it denies an owner command whose input already carries `--expect-sha256`, and `run.mjs` refuses unless each file argument has exactly one hash (r4-TM T4-6). It denies an owner command (a) when the hook input has an `agent_id` (teammate prompts appear in the lead session, agent-teams.md:298), (b) when `permission_mode` (hooks.md:763) is not one of the modes the A-5 record shows to prompt (expected `default` and `auto`: "A hook's "ask" also forces a permission prompt in auto mode", hooks.md:1971), (c) when it is part of a compound command, pipe or substitution. `A3` is offered only after probe A-5 passed in the probe record of §3.4 item 14 (CH-8). In a `-p` session the `ask` cannot be answered and Claude Code denies the call (hooks.md:1961).
   1.1 The owner may stand in as the judge of a gate (CH-10): `run.mjs judge <phase> --decider "<name>" --verdict pass|pass-with-conditions|block --objections <file>` behind the same `ask` writes a `captured verdict` with `agent_type: owner`, which PG-3 accepts. `run.mjs judge` never replaces a devils-advocate capture on a `D-n`; it replaces only a gate's judgment role.
   1.2 Every `run.mjs` call (r4-DA N2, r4-PR R4-5, r5-TM T5-1, r5-DA O5-1, X-1; CH-49): `run.mjs` matches the one `excludedCommands` pattern, and an excluded command goes through the regular permission flow (sandboxing.md:176), so without a hook decision every `enter`, `exit`, `status`, `id`, `rule`, `return`, `halt`, `mode`, `fanout`, `worktree`, `usage` would prompt and stall a run at `A1`–`A3`. `run.mjs` and `guard-shell.mjs` read a call with one parser, `lib/run-argv.mjs`, and one table of allowed forms: each owner-command form of step 1 (the subcommand with the flags that make it one, such as `rule --human`, `init --start`, `init --mode team`, `decision … decided --decider <other than the executor role>`, `probe --accept`) and each routine form (a non-owner subcommand with its allowed flags). Token rule (cc-TM F6-1, cc-DA D-1): the parser accepts an unquoted token only from the charset `[A-Za-z0-9._:/@+=-]` (no `{`, `}`, `$`, backtick, `*`, `?`, `[` or `~`, so no brace, parameter, command, glob or tilde expansion), and a value token otherwise only as one whole single-quoted string or one whole double-quoted string without `$` or backtick; subcommand and flag tokens stay unquoted, and no `--flag=value` form is accepted. `guard-shell.mjs` denies every other form (CDP5 checks only quotes and backslashes in git subcommands, `guard-bash.mjs:368`; an unquoted brace passes such a check, and `run.mjs rule {--human,x} --why y` reaches `run.mjs` as `rule --human x --why y`). Call shape (cc2-TM F7-1 to F7-3): on every shell-running tool (Bash, PowerShell, Monitor; §1.4 item 10) `guard-shell.mjs` denies every call whose text contains `run.mjs`, `--argv-sha256`, `--session-id`, `--data-dir` or `--hook-token` unless the call is exactly `node <P>/scripts/run.mjs` followed by tokens of the table, where `<P>/scripts/run.mjs` is absolute and byte-equal to the path of the `excludedCommands` entry (§5.6 item 6; `<P>` is the hook's `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT`, hooks.md:480): no token before `node` (no variable prefix such as `FOO=1` or `NODE_OPTIONS=…`, no wrapper such as `timeout`, `nice`, `env` or `exec`), no token between `node` and the script path (no node option), no relative script path, and no control character anywhere in the call, so an unquoted newline cannot start a second `run.mjs` call. A read-only command that only names `run.mjs`, such as `grep run.mjs notes.md`, is denied as well; the Grep tool stays available. Whether the `excludedCommands` matcher itself strips a prefix is not in the docs copy (it "matches the text of the call", sandboxing.md:321) and is recorded by probe A-19. Parity is made true, not claimed: `guard-shell.mjs` appends the keyed `--hook-token` of step 1.3 over its parsed, dequoted token list after the script path through the same `updatedInput` as `--session-id` and `--data-dir` (UC-09 step 1); `run.mjs` recomputes it over its own argv without the tokens the hook appends (`--expect-sha256`, `--session-id`, `--data-dir`, `--hook-token`, each with its value) and exits 2 when the two differ, or when the token is missing or appears more than once. The token replaces the plain `--argv-sha256` of v0.4.5, which any caller that knows the session id and data dir could compute (cc2-TM F7-1). Of the token and form checks, `run.mjs` makes only those its argv shows and exits 2 on them: a `--flag=value` form, an unknown subcommand or flag, a duplicate `--session-id`, `--data-dir` or `--hook-token`, and the token mismatch; the shell removes quotes and escapes before argv, so the quoted and escaped cases are tested on `guard-shell.mjs` only. `guard-shell.mjs` decides a `run.mjs` call by that table only, a positive list: deny for a caller with `agent_id`, a compound command, a call the parser rejects, and a subcommand or flag outside the table; the owner `ask` of step 1 for an owner-command form; for a routine form the routine decision with the `updatedInput` of UC-09 step 1. The routine decision is `ask` until SL-8 and `allow` from SL-8: by the consumer test of §11.3 no stage-1 step needs a call without a prompt, because the live run is attended at `A0` (CH-49), and SL-8 builds what unattended levels need (AC-11.15 in SL-1, AC-11.16 in SL-8). A hook `allow` skips the prompt but deny and ask rules of the settings still apply (hooks.md:1960), and an owner command's `ask` wins over any `allow` (hooks.md:1965). Probe A-5 records that the owner's `ask` still prompts beside these `allow`s, by the prompt's `[plugin:cdp6-core]` label (hooks.md:1969).
   1.3 Keyed hook token, a second layer (cc2-TM F7-1 hardening, CH-50): r5-TM T5-1, cc-TM F6-1 and cc2-TM F7-1 are three bypasses of one layer, the `guard-shell.mjs` decision, which was the only thing between a model-made `run.mjs` call and an owner record; `run.mjs` therefore also checks a token that only a hook can make, whether or not the hook saw the call. Key: `bootstrap.mjs` creates `~/.config/cdp6-core/hook.key` at SessionStart when it is absent (32 bytes from `crypto.randomBytes`, directory mode 0700, file created exclusively with mode 0600, never printed or logged). Hooks run outside the sandbox with the user's access (sandboxing.md:43) and `run.mjs` runs unsandboxed as the one excluded command (§5.6 item 6), so both read the key; the model reads it through no tool: sandboxed Bash, PowerShell and Monitor commands are refused by `sandbox.filesystem.denyRead` (sandboxing.md:9, :32, :276), the Read tool, which `denyRead` does not stop (sandboxing.md:42), by a `Read(...)` deny rule (settings.md:143; it also blocks `@` references, hooks.md:1740-1745), Grep by `guard-files.mjs` from SL-4 (UC-13 step 1); the docs copy does not say whether a `Read(...)` rule covers Grep, so probe A-22 records it, and when it does not, SL-1 registers `guard-phase.mjs` on `Read|Grep` with a deny of the key directory for every caller until SL-4 (§11.1 A-22); and no caller can replace the key with a known one, because `denyWrite`, `Edit(...)` and `Write(...)` deny rules and `guard-files.mjs` cover the directory (§5.6 item 9). Token: for every `run.mjs` call it decides with `ask` or `allow`, `guard-shell.mjs` appends `--hook-token <nonce>.<issued>.<mac>`, where `nonce` is 16 fresh random bytes, `issued` the issue time in Unix milliseconds and `mac` HMAC-SHA256 under the key over the nonce, the issue time, the hook input's `session_id`, its `CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA`, the parsed, dequoted token list after the script path and every `--expect-sha256` value the hook appends (step 1), joined by NUL (cc3-TM D-1: the hash values stood outside the MAC); a missing or unreadable key makes the hook deny every `run.mjs` call with the reason. Check: before any other action `run.mjs` exits 2 when the key is unreadable; the token is missing, malformed or appears more than once; the HMAC it computes over the nonce, the issue time, its own `--session-id`, its own `--data-dir`, its argv without the appended tokens and its own `--expect-sha256` values differs (constant-time compare); the issue time is more than ten minutes old (an `ask` answered later fails closed and the call is repeated); the nonce is already in `<data>/hook-nonces.jsonl`; or `process.execArgv` is not empty or `NODE_OPTIONS` is set. Right after the MAC and age checks pass, and before any further check or action, it appends the nonce to `<data>/hook-nonces.jsonl` under the lock it checked it under (cc3-TM D-1), so a token `run.mjs` has seen is spent even when a later check exits 2; a replay through a path the hook does not see must fail, because the modified tool input, token included, can stand in the session transcript (the docs copy does not say it does not; probe A-22 records it). A token issued for a call that never reaches `run.mjs` (the owner declines, the auto classifier denies, hooks.md:1971, another hook denies, hooks.md:1965) has no nonce line: the ten-minute bound limits it, and the `denyRead` and `Read(...)` entries on `~/.claude/projects/` keep the transcript from the model (§5.6 item 9). `run.mjs` never prints, logs or journals the token, also not on exit 2, where it names only the failed check. A tool the hook does not cover, a prefix the parser misses and a token minted for another session or data directory therefore write no owner record (AC-11.17, AC-11.18, SL-1). Residual (cc3-TM D-3, D-4): every process Claude Code starts outside the sandbox with the user's full access (command hooks, a repository's too, local MCP servers, plugin monitors, LSP servers, helper commands; sandboxing.md:43) can read the key and write the run files without a tool call; `guard-net.mjs` denies `mcp__*` calls in an active run only from SL-4 (UC-13 step 3, AC-13.17); stage 1 rests on the owner's host check and stage 2 on the admin-required sandbox (§0 item 20 (2), §7.4).
2. Every decision the orchestrator meets gets a `decision class`: `mechanical` (decide, ruling line), `taste` (decide, ruling line, listed at the final gate), `user challenge` (never decided by a model: STOP-9).
3. Rulings are written with `run.mjs rule "<what>" --why "<why>" --cost "<cost if wrong>" --class <class>` as `R-n` lines; no stall on a decidable point.
   3.1 `run.mjs rule --human "<what>" --why "<why>" [--allow-round S-n]` is the owner's ruling (owner command): it records decider `owner`; with `--allow-round` it lets `guard-spawn.mjs` accept one more worker spawn for that slice (AC-05.5) and moves a `parked` slice back to `briefed` (§5.1).
4. A stop-list item (§6.2) ends the unattended part with a typed `terminal status`; the run state is resumable.
5. An unattended run never exits a phase while a spawned worker of that phase has no captured handback (`counters.json` spawns versus captures), or while a teammate is active (§3.4 item 8).
6. Budgets: at most four concurrent workers per wave and two waves without a recorded owner go, as CDP5 orchestrate; the message budget per team and wave (§3.4 item 4); the token budget is reported by the owner until a source is shown (A-8).

#### Error cases

| Case | Behavior |
|---|---|
| Token budget reached | `HALT-BUDGET` with spent and budget |
| Intent gap (two readings of an AC or a goal) | `HALT-INTENT-GAP`, the readings listed, no reading picked |
| Owner command run in a permission mode the A-5 record does not show to prompt | denied by `guard-shell.mjs` with the mode named (TM-3) |
| Owner command from a caller with `agent_id` | denied (TM2-4) |
| `permission_mode` absent from the hook input | denied (fail closed) until SL-0 probe A-13 shows the field on PreToolUse |
| Exit attempted with an uncaptured worker | refused with the agent ids |

### UC-12: Spawn a role, capture its handback, validate its verdict

**Actor:** `orchestrator`, `operator` or `team lead`; `cdp6-hooks` (`guard-spawn.mjs`, `inject-contract.mjs`, `check-handback.mjs`, `check-stop.mjs`).
**Route:** `Agent` tool with `subagent_type: cdp6-core:<role>`; `SubagentHandback`

#### Behavior

1. The spawn prompt opens with the `brief header` (§5.3) and the line "Read your brief at <path>."; PreToolUse on `Agent` carries `tool_input.prompt`, `description`, `subagent_type`, `model` (r1-team-facts addendum; `name` and `team_name` are read by CDP5, `guard-spawn.mjs:13-14`, and confirmed by probe A-1).
2. `guard-spawn.mjs` denies: in an active run any `subagent_type` not starting with `cdp6-core:` (TM-6); a named spawn outside a phase whose card allows team mode or without a team gate (§3.4 item 1); a missing or malformed header; a brief failing `brief-lint.mjs`; a base diff on cited paths (O7); a third worker spawn or third review round per task (O14); two concurrent spawns of the same role for the same task; a spawn of a role while a pending spawn of the same role is still unbound (TM2-5; same-role workers of a wave are therefore spawned one after another); a concurrent spawn without a fan-out or team record (OR-1); any spawn by a caller with `agent_id` (workers spawn no subagents, C8c); a writer spawn whose `env:` names no worktree registered by `run.mjs worktree` (OR-9, UC-05 step 2). It records a pending spawn (task, role, `target:` hashes, time) in `counters.json`. A pending spawn with no `SubagentStart` before the caller's next PreToolUse is marked `void` (another hook denied the `Agent` call, hooks.md:1965, or the prompt was declined); a void spawn counts as no spawn, no round and no reconciliation gap (r3-KR N8; the order is part of probe A-18).
3. `inject-contract.mjs` injects `CDP6-CONTRACT v1` at SubagentStart (as `cdp5-agents/hooks/inject-contract.mjs:21-25`) and binds the one unbound pending spawn of that `agent_type` to the `agent_id` from the hook input (hooks.md:2541 input; KR-10); because step 2 allows at most one unbound pending spawn per role, the binding does not rest on start order (A-18 records the order for concurrent spawns of different roles). For teammates see §3.4 item 2.
4. At `SubagentHandback` (`check-handback.mjs`, as `cdp5-agents/hooks/check-handback.mjs:46-72` reads `tool_input.message` and `agent_id`): acknowledgement, the `run:`/`task:` line, whose task must equal the bound spawn's task (a mismatch denies), hedges, written-file claims (each `wrote: <path>` line names a file whose mtime is later than the spawn time), PII lines; first failure denies once with the reason. When the contract cannot be verified the handback is denied, not passed (CDP5's branch at `check-handback.mjs:54-61` passes it; CDP6 does not copy that).
5. The accepted message is written to `R/verdicts/<task>-<role>-<agent-id>.md` with sha256, the bound spawn's `target:` hashes and an index line (`captured verdict`); `verdict-check.mjs` validates role outputs (O16): mapping line, `fresh context: yes`, `rejected alternative provided: yes` for devils-advocate (a pass with `no` is invalid, orchestrate SKILL.md:68), `conflict of interest:` line (C13; a line that names a stake records the capture `conflicted`, STOP-5, whatever its verdict; `verdict-check.mjs` marks it `cleared` only on a later capture whose bound `target:` lines include the conflicted capture's `target:` hash, whose `agent_type` differs from the conflicted role, whose own `conflict of interest:` line reads `none`, whose bound spawn's brief header carried `clears: <capture id of the conflicted capture>` (§5.3), and whose output has one `disposition:` line per conflicted item, that is per objection `F-Pk-n` and per premise of the conflicted capture (premises cited as `<capture id>:premise-<n>`), or on `run.mjs object` records per item (owner decision §0 item 19 (2), r5-TM T5-3, r5-DA X-2); the clearing capture's own verdict counts at the gate like any capture; the clearing branch is built in SL-5, and until then only the `run.mjs object` records clear), premises section (O4), every `block` objection traceable; a devils-advocate verdict "not decidable without <evidence>" is recorded as no pass, so `decision-check.mjs`, which accepts only (a) and (a) with conditions, keeps the `D-n` open until a later round passes (UC-03 step 3.2).
6. `check-stop.mjs` covers workers that end without the handback tool (`decision: block` once, `stop_hook_active` on the second pass, as `cdp5-agents/hooks/check-ack.mjs:13,61`).

#### Error cases

| Case | Behavior |
|---|---|
| Named spawn outside a team-mode phase or without team gate | denied (the contract would not be bound, the counters would not see it) |
| Contract not verifiable at handback | handback denied; the run halts with STOP-8 |
| Counter-role verdict `block` without an `F-Pk-n` objection that cites an artefact id and a file:line or command | `verdict-check.mjs` rejects the capture; the orchestrator re-runs the role |
| Devils-advocate output without a premise list when the brief named code paths | rejected (O4) |
| Verdict's target hash differs from the current artefact | capture marked `stale`; the gate does not count it (O15) |
| Counter-role agent id appears as writer of its target in the tool log | capture rejected: no fresh context |

| AC-12.20 | A pending spawn followed by the caller's next PreToolUse without a `SubagentStart` is marked `void` and does not count as spawn, round or reconciliation gap | Unit | `hooks/guard-spawn.test.mjs` "fires: phantom pending spawn" | red |
| AC-12.21 | A devils-advocate capture with verdict (a) and `conflict of interest: author of D-2` is recorded `conflicted`; a second capture of a fresh devils-advocate instance does not clear it; `run.mjs object` records for every objection and premise of it do | Unit | `checks/verdict-check.test.mjs` "fires: declared stake counted as pass", "fires: fresh same-role instance clears", "clears on object records per item" | red |
| AC-12.22 | In mode `multi` with an A-14 fixture, one `status` message to the worker of a captured handback is allowed and counted, a second is denied; without the A-14 fixture the first is denied | Unit | `hooks/guard-message.test.mjs` "fires: second status message" | red |
| AC-12.23 | An `Agent` call from a caller with `agent_id` (an `impl-uc` worker) is denied | Unit | `hooks/guard-spawn.test.mjs` "fires: worker spawns agent" | red |
| AC-12.24 | A writer spawn whose `env:` names no worktree registered by `run.mjs worktree` is denied, and `run.mjs worktree S-1 <path>` without a `worktree-setup.mjs` tool-log line with exit 0 for that path, or with a path outside `<project root>/.worktrees/`, exits 2 | Unit | `hooks/guard-spawn.test.mjs` "fires: writer without registered worktree"; `scripts/run.test.mjs` "fires: worktree registered on red baseline", "fires: worktree path outside .worktrees" | red |
| AC-12.25 | A subagent handback whose first line is not the contract acknowledgement is denied once by `check-handback.mjs` with the reason | Unit | `hooks/check-handback.test.mjs` "fires: missing acknowledgement" | red |
| AC-12.26 | A `cdp6-core:` spawn whose prompt lacks the brief header, or whose brief fails `brief-lint.mjs`, is denied by `guard-spawn.mjs` | Unit | `hooks/guard-spawn.test.mjs` "fires: spawn without brief header", "fires: spawn with failing brief" | red |
| AC-12.27 | A worker that ends without the handback tool gets one `check-stop.mjs` block, and on the second pass (`stop_hook_active`) none | Unit | `hooks/check-stop.test.mjs` "fires: end without handback" | red |
| AC-12.28 | A `conflicted` devils-advocate capture C on `target:` hash H is not cleared by a later `cdp6-core:konzept-reviewer` capture whose `conflict of interest:` line names a stake, by one without that line, by one on another `target:` hash, by one stamped before it, by a `cdp6-core:review-verify` capture on H with `none` whose brief has no `clears: C`, or by one with `clears: C` that lacks a `disposition:` line for one objection or premise of C; a later `cdp6-core:review-verify` capture on H with `none`, `clears: C` and a disposition line per item clears it, also when each `resolved` disposition cites a `file:line` of the artefact at hash H and no artefact of a cycle `c+1` exists; `brief-lint.mjs` fails a brief whose `clears:` names a capture that is not `conflicted`, has another `target:` hash, or was made by the brief's own role | Unit | `checks/verdict-check.test.mjs` "fires: clearing capture with stake", "fires: clearing capture without conflict line", "fires: clearing capture on other target", "fires: clearing capture before conflicted one", "fires: same-hash review-verify without clears line", "fires: clearing capture without disposition per item", "clears with resolved disposition citing target artefact"; `checks/brief-lint.test.mjs` "fires: clears line names no conflicted capture" | red |

### UC-13: Guard files, shell and network

**Actor:** `cdp6-hooks` (`guard-files.mjs`, `guard-shell.mjs`, `guard-net.mjs`, `trajectory.mjs`).
**Route:** PreToolUse `guard-files.mjs` on `Read|Grep|Glob|Edit|Write|NotebookEdit|MultiEdit`; `guard-shell.mjs` on `Bash|PowerShell|Monitor` (§1.4 item 10; this hook also handles `curl`, `wget`, `gh` and `git` remote commands); `guard-net.mjs` on `WebFetch|WebSearch|mcp__.*`; `trajectory.mjs` on PostToolUse and PostToolUseFailure on all tools in an active run

#### Behavior

1. `guard-files.mjs` for `cdp6-core:` agents and teammates: judgment roles write nothing; writers write only the paths their brief header names in `writes:` (their worktree, their report) (O13); every agent: no read of private stores (path patterns of contract rule C8d) or of secret files (`~/.ssh`, `~/.aws`, `.env*`; the sandbox's `denyRead` does not cover the Read tool, sandboxing.md:42), no write to plugin state, `<data>/`, the pointer directory `~/.cache/cdp6-core/` (r5-DA O5-2), `run.json`, `counters.json`, `verdicts/`, `tool-log.jsonl`, `team-log.jsonl`, `.git/`, `~/.claude/teams/`, hook files and settings; no write to `<data>/`, `~/.cache/cdp6-core/` or `docs/cdp6/probes/` and no Read, Grep, Glob or write of the key directory `~/.config/cdp6-core/` (UC-11 step 1.3) by any caller in any session in which `cdp6-core` is enabled, and none under the `cdp6-core` and `cdp5-gates` plugin roots by any caller in a run (§3.4 item 14); no write to the run's `--settings` file (§5.6) by any caller; no write under a banned path (§6.5) by any caller in any mode. Unknown callers per §1.4 item 2 are denied.
   1.1 Main session in an active run: deny writes to the state files above and to `<data>/hooks-fired.jsonl`; deny Read, Grep and Glob of secret paths (`~/.ssh`, `~/.aws`, `~/.config/gh`, `.env*`; TM2-3); `ask` on hook files and `.claude/settings*.json` (STOP-2). In mode `single` during P5 the main session writes only the current slice's worktree and `R/` outside the state files (KR-8).
2. `guard-shell.mjs` applies the CDP5 tiers (copied from `cdp5-agents/hooks/guard-bash.mjs`) plus: CDP6 state paths; deny of every Bash call with `dangerouslyDisableSandbox: true` from any caller in an active run (sandboxing.md:210 names it the escape hatch); deny of `curl`, `wget` and `gh api` for writers whatever the method (CDP5 lets GET through, `guard-bash.mjs:452-455`); deny of the sending flags of `curl` and `wget` (data, form, upload and a method other than GET or HEAD) for the main session in an active run, since a network write is STOP-3 (r5-TM residual); deny of `git commit`, `push`, `fetch` and history rewrites for agents (C8a); deny of any command naming a file under the `cdp6-core` `scripts/` or `checks/` directory for a caller with `agent_id`, so no worker or teammate runs `run.mjs` or a checker, which the unsandboxed `run.mjs` pattern would record as an orchestrator action (r3-TM TM3-3; CDP5 denies its orchestrator scripts to workers the same way, `guard-bash.mjs:112-114`); deny or `ask` on the irreversible steps of STOP-1 for the main session (OR-24); the owner-command rules of UC-11 step 1; the call-shape rule of UC-11 step 1.2, which denies a compound, prefixed, wrapped or relative `run.mjs` call (the exact `run.mjs` pattern is the only `excludedCommands` entry, §5.6 item 6); deny of the fourth identical command after three identical failures, and of a write that repeats an A-B-A-B content oscillation (O17).
3. `guard-net.mjs` (TM-2, TM2-3, TM2-6), every caller in an active run: `WebFetch` only for an exact URL registered in `run.json` `sources.urls` by `run.mjs allow-url <url>|--file <list>` (owner command; a list is shown in one `ask`); `WebSearch` denied unless the owner set `search: allowed` with `run.mjs allow-search` (owner command; r3-DA O-6); every `mcp__*` tool denied (an MCP need is STOP-2). Bash network commands are bounded by the sandbox (§5.6), which covers `node -e`, `python3 -c` and any other client the command-name tripwire of `guard-shell.mjs` misses. CDP6 does not use `WebFetch(domain:…)` allow rules for this, because they also pre-allow the domain for every sandboxed command (sandboxing.md:586-587).
4. `trajectory.mjs` (PostToolUse and PostToolUseFailure) appends one line per tool call to `R/tool-log.jsonl`, for agents, teammates and, in an active run, the main session: agent id or `main`, tool, `tool_use_id`, `permission_mode`, for file tools the path, for `WebFetch` the URL, for Bash the command's sha256 and, when it calls a script under the `cdp6-core` `scripts/` directory, the script name and its path arguments, exit status, time (r5-KR G-B; consumers: §3.4 item 12.1, AC-16.19, AC-05.19, AC-12.24, AC-02.11). The exit status of a failed shell command comes from the PostToolUseFailure `error` field ("Exit code 1 …", hooks.md:2279-2324); a call seen only by PostToolUse counts as exit 0.
5. PreToolUse `tool_input`: Edit and Write carry an absolute `file_path` (r1-team-facts addendum); MultiEdit and NotebookEdit are not documented and are confirmed by probe A-2 or denied for agents.

#### Error cases

| Case | Behavior |
|---|---|
| Unparseable hook input | exit 2, `fail closed` (as `guard-bash.mjs:672-688`) |
| Path with `..` escaping the worktree | resolved before the check; deny |
| Symlink inside the worktree pointing outside | real path checked; deny |
| PostToolUseFailure `error` without an "Exit code" prefix | line written with `exit: unknown`; `tdd-order.mjs` treats unknown as not red |
| Tool not documented for hooks (MultiEdit, NotebookEdit) used by an agent | denied until probe A-2 confirms its `file_path` field |

#### Acceptance criteria

|---|---|---|---|---|
| AC-13.1 | `review-verify` Write to any path is denied | Unit | `hooks/guard-files.test.mjs` "fires: judgment write" | red |
